Summary

"Good." I was going to get my breakfast made for me by a compiler, like any good independent terran should. "New hab. Will you be mandating that I have a tour, or am I good to map it out myself?"

"It's your choice. Though, I did give my little Gaia a tour of this hab, perhaps you should figure it out on your own?" she offered. A choice, a fork in the road. Which was going to make me seem more independent? On one hand, asking to be shown around the hab might demonstrate that I was leaning on her too much. On the other, if I got lost while touring, she'd definitely start believing that I'm helpless and decide to renege on her promise. I absolutely couldn't ask her to make the choice for me, that'd be beyond the pale.

Turn one twist downwards of the spiral, you had access to the bathroom. Another and it was the living room. There was also a few twists further down, but I didn't feel like investigating them at this point. There also seemed to be a balcony if you went directly upwards from Lana's room, which was interesting.

The living room was comfortable enough. A wooden table greeted me in the middle of the room with a high chair on one end, a more standard chair next to it, and a much bigger one presumably for Lana. A compiler was to the side with a kitchenette underneath it, and TV to the far side with a sofa opposite it. Comfortable cream colours that you'd expect in any city apartment greeted me. "Huh. That is quite weird." Damn it, she got another chuckle from me. "Should you be T-posing in the other room, waiting for Gaia to be back?"

"T-posing? I don't believe I understand that concept. Care to explain?" Right. She's an alien.

"Never mind." Pathing to the compiler, I began my order. "Hab, I want a breakfast burrito with hash browns, sausages, and ketchup. Oh, and hot sauce?" A buzzing noise was my reward, squinting in utter disbelief as the machine flashed red.

"Is that how little girls like you are meant to ask for what they want?" I stared at it in a mix of bewilderment, holding my attention at the panel for a good moment. Maybe if I gave it a funny enough look it'd revert. "Oh! Miss Provins, is this not Gaia?"

"Oh, right. Probably should've set it to standard English. One second…" With a few presses on a tablet she decidedly did not have earlier, the computer made an affirming beeping noise. "I got caught up in the rush of the morning, I apologise."

"I'm sorry too, Ella. I wouldn't tolerate that use of language from sweet baby Gaia, but you're a big girl who can say what she wants!" Perhaps the most infuriating part of this entire affair is how infantilizing it was even while confirming that I wasn't a bed-wetter. Sighing, I received my breakfast burrito, sitting down at the table.

"Please don't let that happen again," I whined.

"Oh, well. I suppose since you're asking so nicely." I suppressed a groan, instead letting it pass painlessly through my body with the ease you'd deliver a kidney stone. I took the first bite into my burrito, delighting in the savoury mix of carbs and meat. The spicy sauce really married the flavours together, creating a smooth aftertaste.

"Always forget how good these things are on the morning." Don't eat too quickly though. Not around her. "So, I've got my bedroom, correct? I'm not going to be sleeping next to you everyday."

"Of course. One twist below the living room, there's a room just for you and or any guests that you might bring over."

"That's a relief. I was worried I'd have to relieve this every night." I decidedly stopped myself from thinking about it.

"I don't want to sleep in the same bed as someone who isn't my floret. Only special girls get that treatment." There was some smugness inflected in her tone when she said that.

"I just share a body with your special girl. Got it."

"Exactly." She put her elbows on the desk, resting her chin on her hands. "Would you want to sleep in the same bed as your friend?" There was absolutely no way Lana didn't know me and Sarah shared a bed when she stayed over. Or perhaps she was just this clueless.

"So, we're friends now?" Don't rise to it. Just take a bite of your burrito after speaking.

"Well. I'd call us roommates more than anything else at the moment. A marriage of convenience rather than an actual relationship. Your body holds my darling baby. In return for access to her, I shall give you space to breathe."

"I understand. It's best for both of us if we're friendly with each other." One last bite, and my food was gone. I somewhat wished to compile another one. I took the time to study Lana afterwards, decidedly curious now about her anatomy now that I was safely next to one of these plants. "Nice… dress thing? I" I pointed at her skirt.

"Oh? This?" Lana stood up with a frustratingly pleasant array of vines rustling sounds. "I modelled this body after a typically feminine woman. I thought this 'dress' of flora was a nice touch to it. It also allows me to do this." She took her hands to either side of the hem, curtseying promptly after.

"I did wonder why so many Affini are entirely naked. I simply thought that was what they enjoyed." Each of her vines danced and waved about in ways that could entrap me if I wasn't intentionally keeping myself focused on her. I had written seven pages on their biorhythm, I wasn't about to fall victim to it now.

"We choose the form to best please the species we're interacting with. You should see the Spectrum Jelly worlds. They're all straggly and wavy," Lana chuckled, deconstructing her arms to make them reminiscent of those creatures before dancing them back and forth.

"Huh. Neat." I decided to start texting instead of keeping talking to her. Switching my profile from Gaia's to mine, I begun texting with both thumbs.

Something I hadn't accounted for. I needed to figure this out. It made sense. Frustrating, complete, and utter sense.

"So…the implant? You'll be putting one in us eventually, right?" Even the concept that such a thing was going anywhere near my body made my hair stick on end in fear. Still, I had to walk this tightrope.

I had wrote extensively on what an implant meant. Spent so much time pouring through what exactly it does through veterinary records and firsthand accounts from affected sophonts. The implant was always the final nail in the coffin, you were at that point hopelessly dependent on your owner. The idea that that thing could be in my spine as an independent was harrowing. It made my back wince in agony even thinking about it.

"Of course not. I had a feeling you didn't want to be in nappies for the rest of your life," she smugly sung back. I sighed.

Secondly, there was a shower and non-floret shampoo and body wash. Guess she was being truthful about that.

Finally, the bathroom was filled with foresty affects. This was definitely inspired by nature, with vines and flowers snaking across marble walls. I couldn't tell if they were synthetic or real, but the effect it created was impressive. Not my touch, though. I preferred the marble.

I sighed, flicking my phone up. Okay. About two weeks. Let's make it work.

Chapter 4: Reconnaissance

Chapter Notes

CW for how someone lost their eye and non-con at the end

One thing that I was always proud of myself for was the ability to hit the ground running. Maintaining a measured composure during such difficulties wasn't the easiest task in the world, yet it was what I did best. My headmate had just been domesticated? Fine. I can handle that. I'm going to dust myself off and keep running.

The coffee shop that I frequented was nestled in the shopping centre, overlooking a balcony of the rest of the complex. It was buried between an ice cream shop I've been told Gaia adores and an Italian restaurant where I usually go. Me and Penny had actually met not too far away from here, a few years back, but it was only since the signing of the Domestication Treaty that we had dated.

Guess I had to thank the Affini for something.

I was earlier than expected, on account of my not wanting to be in the hab any longer. Settling my elbows against the railing of the balcony, I observed the sophonts and Affini wandering about. By rough estimations it appeared that about 33% of the Terrans in this subsection underneath me had an owner, with a neat cut of about 80% of those walking around with their affini. Even the ones who weren't collared acted so naturally around the xenos nowadays. I briefly pondered just what was going on in the floret's heads, if any of them were suffering with something similar.

Of course not. I doubt many of them had someone else in their head making decisions about their body. Maybe some of them, given the implant and whatever nonsense florets got up to.

I was technically a floret, wasn't I? Was I? This entire situation felt confusing. Lana had informed me that an affini wasn't about to attempt to domesticate me because of Gaia, but where did that leave me? What would happen in a fortnight when there was plant matter in my spine?

Speaking of, there was an unsettling feeling that scratched at the back of my mind when Lana was around, something… notably different. I couldn't decipher if it was some part of her or feedback from Gaia, but it wasn't pleasant. It was utterly unlike anything I've ever experienced before.

Biorhythm, perhaps? It was decidedly difficult for an affini to turn it down completely. Then again, it could potentially just be Gaia clawing out, reaching for her 'Mummy'. Before I left for the day, she made me read the domestication contract the two had signed. It made sense for a non-typical domestication to have a non-typical contract, but the deviation was far too great for my liking. Still, I did like a legally binding document declaring my independence.

Though, it did make me wonder if this was another trick? Why would she lay down all of her cards at once? She had to be planning something. But what? Was I really in a grand chess game with an affini, or was I just shadowboxing someone's genuine intentions?

Interrupting my runaway train of thoughts before it barrelled into a ditch was Penny, her voice slicing from the crowd behind me. She always had a distinct voice that cut through any noise.

"Ella!" She cried out, forcing me to snap my head to her. "There's my girl! It's been too long!" The tanned woman was bolting towards me, demanding me to open my arms lest I get overran with the tackle hug that she was surely going for.

I have no idea how I ended up with this girl. Even before Class-Gs she was decidedly out of my league. She was active, fighting fit, and charismatic. The kind of woman that seemed to know everyone everywhere and had friends on every corner. Yet, she saw something in me and Gaia. I wasn't sure what it was, but after six months of being together proper I had learnt to trust in her instinct on this one. Long, black hair was tied neatly into a ponytail with a brown puffer jacket adorning her upper body and black skinny jeans. Her sublime features were outfitted with a brown bark eye patch, leaving one good brown eye. She lost her right eye during her military days, something about incident with protesters.

Well, that was the last story she had told me. No one knew the how and why exactly. She liked to keep people guessing as to what actually happened, from the most mundane and obviously fake to terrifying amounts of detail. Once she told someone that she plucked it out accidentally when she fell on an ice cream scoop. The next day it was that she had lost in the conflict of 2550 against a horde of Rinans, one of them stabbing her eye with a throwing knife.

She kept this up even with me and Gaia. Maybe she just enjoyed telling stories. I always enjoyed listening to them. Her voice was pretty.

"Penny! Been way too long!" She flung herself at me, collecting her in a giant hug. Penny was a few centimetres taller than me but she always angled herself to be on my chest, adorably. "How's my amazing girl been?"

"Been good! Was nice catching up with the kiddo and my other girls." She unfurled her arms around my shoulders, pressing a gentle poke into my stomach. "Anyway! How's life as a floret treating ya?" I rolled my eyes broadly and dramatically at her, kissing her tenderly on the cheek soon after. Just like that, I felt a resounding calm resonate in my body. I didn't deserve this girl.

"I'm not the floret in this body. That's little miss bed wetter, not yours truly." I attempted to shine with bravado, holding up a thumb. "Still Ella Lockheart, and definitely not a pet." Her facial expression went from bemused to that look she usually gave me when she was staring at me. If I could hazard a guess it was adoration, probably. She then cocked her head a bit with a chuckle.

"Gaia Provins and Ella Lockheart, sharing a body. That's precious." She swooped a peck on my lips, taking my hand forcefully to the shop. "C'mon, Miss Independent. We need to get coffee."

"I'm quite proud of my second name, thank you. I picked it myself." I stood my ground, juxtaposing my verbal defiance with my physical movement.

Previously in the Accord, several coffee shops attempted to present the illusion of classiness. This was done by 'fancy' furnished imitation wood panelling, jazz music spilling out from tinny speakers. Now, there was actual class in these places. Not quite fancy enough that you'd see a waiter, but definitely not somewhere you'd see a punk.

We both grabbed our coffee and complimentary confectionary, myself opting for a latte with two pumps of salted caramel syrup and an almond croissant. Penny grabbed a brownie and an Irish coffee. I briefly questioned the alcoholic drink, but knew that wouldn't scratch her.

"So… tell me more about the lucky lady." Penny inquired as we sat down, and I merely quirked an eyebrow at her. "The affini that got Gaia? She's really lucky to have such a sweet girl as her as her pet."

"You're just saying that because you think I'm sweet and we share a body, Penny." I giggled back with a hint of a blush. Just a little twinge, though. "Not sure. she's an affini? Based off a classical rose, despite being named after a French one. Flourishes her body with a very pretty dress. I make a point of not staring at her too much, if I can help it." Rule five of interacting with the Affini.

"Oooh, so you think she's pretty? Why you so afraid of staring at her? She's got you anyway, you don't need to worry about getting double domesticated." She grabbed my cheek and squeezed it a bit, making me groan in pain. "And duh, of course I think Gaia's sweet! Both of you are absolutely adorable and I love my girlfriends." She gifted two kisses, one on the 'Ella' side of my face, another on the 'Gaia' side.

"Kind of a moot point. She's an affini, of course she's pretty. They're walking cognitohazards that want us to pack bond with them." I was attempting to seem nonplussed, but with how much affection Penny liked to deliver on me in such excess it was pretty impossible.

"Seems like it worked for a certain babygirl." She relinquished the grasp on my cheek, only to grab my hand again. "Okay, okay. Gonna stop messing with you, but would you fuck her?"

"Excuse me!?" I was glad I didn't have a drink in my mouth otherwise it'd be all over her face by now. This response only served to summon a volley of laughter from my girlfriend, loud enough to turn a few heads at the establishment.

"C'mon, you haven't seen those wandering masses of plant matter and thought about fucking them at least once? They've been here six months, I thought it about it the first day!"

"So why haven't you? What's stopping little Miss Popular from walking up to an affini and asking for vines in her ass?"

"Why haven't I gotten into bed with an affini?" She shrugged broadly. "Same reason you won't. I'm worried they'd be like a clingy ex and want to slip a collar on me." It was hard to imagine her with a collar on. Even conjuring the image in my head felt like a bad hack job with an image editing software.

"So, it's a trust thing that prevents you from shagging an alien?"

"Kinda? I mean, look, if I found the right affini I wouldn't mind being a pet. Just, I wanna experience it without worrying about them taking me."

"And the whole… making you utterly dependent on them? That's the thing that scares me." A hint of fear dipped under my voice, definitely caught by her.

"I was on medical leave in the military once. When my leg got shot to all fuck, I had to have someone wipe my ass and change my bedpans for me. I'm no stranger to depending on someone." It was even harder to picture Penny of all people requiring bathing by someone else.

"Sounds like you've got this figured." I shook my head a few times with a soft sigh. "Still, I can't believe your first response to alien invasion of our species was 'can I fuck it?' You know you can see something and not want to fuck it, right? Do you see a dog and think that, huh?" I jabbed her in the side of her hand playfully.

"Weeeeell, all I'm saying is if I found a puppy girl I'd probably beg her owner to fuck her while she's leashed." Penny was so cheery about this, while I just had to unleash a pent up sigh.

"I find it hard to believe you of all girls cannot find a single puppy girl in this city. There must be dozens of them in any club."

"You'd think that, but nah. Lots of cat girls, but puppies?" She poked my cheek. She really, really liked touching me. "'There's a lack of puppy girls in this city. Someone should really fix that."

I sometimes wondered if she was an affini in disguise. Perhaps under all that skin was a mass of plant matter, just waiting to stick an injector into me. No, she had known me for six months. She had ample amounts of opportunities to inject me.

"So," I cleared my throat like a sharp jab into the conversation. "I'm not a puppy or an age regressor. What's so interesting about me?"

"You're cute and pretty and real smart." Another swift kiss. "You're also the only girl in this city who treats me like just another girl, y'know? So many girls I've slept with treat me like I'm this prize, this… thing to be worshipped. All because I'm hot and I top." She spoke like that was her only redeeming qualities while she swigged down her coffee.

"Well, glad to know my intelligence is attractive. Do you think I'd suit glasses, babe?"

"Fuck no, they'd make you look insufferable."

My time with Penny came and went, and soon it was off to the second meeting today. From my girlfriend to my best friend, Sarah Victor. I knew her since our college days, bonding over our shared love of card games and her meticulous attention to detail. She was always the competitive player, the one to eke out any edge no matter how small. That interest was turned towards the Affini when they arrived, both of us absolutely fascinated by how to interact in the most optimal fashion to escape domestication.

Truth be told, I personally wasn't afraid of becoming a floret. It was that I had escaped it for so long that it had become a point of pride. The majority of sophonts were not drugged addled pets like some feralists would have you believe, they were happy independents. I was one such individual up until a week ago.

Likewise, it wasn't difficult to remain independent. After the initial rush to grab a floret, most of the affini that might want to break someone already had their claim. Of course, some transplants moved to the city, ravenous for a pet, but those were rare.

I couldn't help wondering if it wasn't for Gaia, I'd still be wandering around in my old place. Doing the same things I usually did.

I missed the normal. Even Penny was treating me just a bit different. No doubt Sarah was going to react wildly when she heard the news. There was a massive plant xeno in my home that was acting like she was my friend. Still, I needed to tell Sarah in person.

Her hab was actually in the same apartment complex as my old one, requiring a tram transfer from the shopping centre. We actually picked habs next to each other so we wouldn't ever be too far apart. It was further away from my- Lana's place than I'm comfortable with, though. Approaching her door, I braced myself with a deep breath in before knocking. Four times, then make a chime to let her know it's really me.

"Doot doot." I melodically sung out.

The door flung over to reveal her. A slightly chubby woman with a taller than average build, Sarah still looked sharp despite it. Long red hair flowed effortlessly down her shoulders and back, complimenting the graphic tee and black jeans she liked to wear. She gave me a friendly hug, quite unlike the full frontal assault I got from Penny earlier.

"I'm so fucking glad to see you, Ella. What took you so long that you were gone a week?" She patted my shoulder a few times with a nervous laugh. "They didn't get you, right? You're still a Lockheart, I don't have to meet someone's owner?"

I gave her a warm smile back and tried my best to remain positive. "It's… a long story. We've got a lot of work to do to update this guide, but I believe we can do it. Should we get sat down first?"

There was one rule with Sarah. You never said anything big you wanted to say before food. Her favourite pizza order was ham, mushrooms, and pepperoni, mine was anchovies and chicken. We sat on the long arm chairs of her dining room table that was clearly refurbished from a previous owner, I made sure to eat a slice before beginning.

"Gaia got florted." I scratched the back of my head anxiously as I told, unable to make eye contact as I spoke. "Turns out there's been an affini waiting in the shadows for when I stopped fronting. Swooped right down and took her the moment I wasn't out." I knew when to slot my eyes back to her, attempting to look as determined as I could.

I need to set the example here. You need to lead her.

"Huh?" Sarah tilted her head a bit at me. Cute little quirk that she did when confused. "You just said you were still a Lockheart. Was… that a lie?" I was afraid of this, the fear in her tone. I watched her begin to worry, her eyebrows furrowing and smile shrinking. For a second, dread begun to run down my spine in sympathetic parallel.

"I'm still a Lockheart." I eventually a returned with a decisive shake of my head. "I'm still independent. The only difference is that there's an affini at my house now. She's being cordial to me for the most part." I shrugged, finding myself tapping a finger upon the desk as I thought. A short pause followed my words. "Though, you know what this gives us?" I leaned in my chair with a smirk.

Sarah furrowed her brow in thought. "A best friend who's a floret in case the affini come by knocking? Might help with the feralist accusations I keep getting."

"Never knew you and Gaia were that close. How charming." I snickered back. "No. It gives us a golden opportunity. We have an affini who's not aggressive to either of us. We can field questions. Ask about the best methods. And now? We have in field experience." Sarah looked at me for a while, tapping a finger rhythmically against the mirrored finger as she thought. I could only imagine the amount of scenarios playing out in her head. Then, she let a long sigh which sounded like her soul was being taken with it.

"And what good is the guide we're making if we can't even save one sophont from being domesticated?" She shifted in her chair, looking towards the nearby window. I couldn't tell if she was looking at the window or through it. "I know Gaia. She followed our notes religiously."

"You're coming at this the wrong way, Sarah. We don't want to be domesticated. Gaia did. We can't help someone who's going to throw themselves at the vine of any affini who wants them, but us?" I tapped at my chest to emphasise my point, leaning forward to her. "We can make a difference. We can write the best guide possible. We can help as many sophonts avoid a collar as we can."

Sarah eventually ripped her gaze from the window to me. She put down her slice of pizza, crossed her legs, and cupped her chin in her thumb. "Fine. So, does this plant at your place know about the guide?"

"Called it 'utter rubbish'." I inflected Lana's accent to increase the mocking.

"Then, we'll show her 'utter rubbish'. They won't know what hit them." I saw that fire burn behind Sarah's eyes. That motivation that was at its most brilliant when she was backed into a corner. "We'll update the guide, make it better. I need you to gather as many notes on Affini biology and behaviour. Should be easy now that you live with one, huh?" A hint of a smile dared to show on her face.

"Absolutely. We can start with what I've noticed this morning, and every day I'll keep you posted on any new information. How they act, how they talk, I'll keep you updated on anything I find."

Sarah reached out a closed fist to me. "We'll knock it out of the park this time. Gaia may be a floret, but us? We'll never have a collar around our neck." She sounded so sure of herself. So confident in her ability.

It was infectious. I felt a surge of energy ricochet from her, jolting me to life.

"I promise you. On both of our prides, neither of us will end up in collars." I bumped her fist back hard enough to hurt us both, the pair of us rejoicing in our declaration.

"Anyway, we've got a long night of updating the guide, don't we? Let's finish this pizza and get to work."

I was in for a long night of theorycrafting, researching, and discussing stories with florets and independents alike. But, since the first time I had started fronting this morning, I felt genuine hope well in my chest.

We'd make it through this ordeal, together, as independents. It was hard not to believe Sarah when she got like this.

"Alright, think we can get Ye-jun? What about his boyfriend? Wait, do you think Penny will be any use here?" Sarah was furiously writing on a keyboard, filling an empty screen with hurried thoughts.

"You're not going to like this, but I think the first sophont we should hit up isn't even a sophont. Lana's probably our best starting point."

"Fuck." Sarah groaned like she had been punched in the chest. "Her?"

The Hab AI greeted me chirpily as I came back, the sound of artificial noise slightly more pleasing than silence currently.

"Welcome back, Ella Lockheart. Should I let Lana know you are home?"

How cute it was that this machine was using my full name.

"It's… fine. I'm sure I'll see her sooner or later." Because of how the layout was structured, you had to go through the living room and then down towards my bedroom. To my dismay, she was sat at the end of the living room table, expecting me. I offered her a bitter glance, she returned with something that approached sympathy.

"You're probably wondering why I just did what I did, correct?" I balled my hands into fists, teeth gritting against one another. I let the fury ride through me, releasing it at my feet like a bolt of lightning. "I assure you, I was trying to minimise the harm."

Chapter 7: Bad Ideas

Chapter Notes

CW for blood, physical violence, suicide mentions.

See the end of the chapter for more notes

I didn't feel good. A swirling unease that gently grasped at my mind like shadowy tendrils of …something, yet the feeling wasn't mine to understand. I could venture into it, yet it was obscured in fog too thick to make clear.

I sighed. Rolling over to an empty bed, I looked for our phone. We weren't meant to check each other's messages, very clearly written rule that we weren't to snoop around in each other's business. Yet…

I briefly checked the time, too. 10:00, about one and a half hours later than I usually wake up. Ella must have slept really late, which meant something really bad had to have happened.

I needed to know. I needed to be a bad girl for a bit.

"Sorry, Ella," I said to myself as I swiped it open. Scanning through her messages, it was quickly apparent what was happening. Sarah had been given a wardship by Lana, something that everyone else understood had zero chance of working. Whether anyone wanted it, Sarah was going to end up a floret by the end of the month, probably. This had resulted in both her and Ella spiralling, and…

"Why is she so afraid of becoming a floret?" I wondered to myself aloud, putting the phone away. I couldn't fathom it. Both of them were so weird about the affini. We lived with one nowadays! I mean, it had only been a week, but still!

That reminds me, I should definitely go to Mummy. I need a collar and a nappy and like, five hugs. Maybe I should ask her if we can install a button in Ella's room that tells her I'm fronting? Call it the Mummy button and it makes some Mummy sounds. Wait, now I'm just thinking about the Egyptian ones.

Wait, if an affini was pure white, would it look like bandages? Was there any pure white flowers? I had to search for it real quick.

"Oh no, you've been affected by the curse of the Mummy! You're now 'doomed' to become a floret, oh nooooo!" I tittered to myself, ascending the staircase while I searched it up. There was quite a few historically, though most had become extinct in the wild thanks to a certain group of Terrans prioritising investor capital over the health of the planet. At least the reforesting and terraforming projects seemed to be going along well!

Wait, there was voices in the living the room. By the sounds of it, Penny? She was muttering something to Mummy. I gently peeked my head through the bottom of the stairs, trying to observe the two of them without being caught.

"Little one, did no one teach you it was rude to eavesdrop?" Mummy informed me that I had been caught almost instantly. I simply presented myself to them both with a 'defeated' sigh.

"I never said I was good at stealth! I may possess many talents, but sneaking is not one of them!" I boasted like I hadn't just failed the stealth check.

"If it made any difference, you fooled me," Penny said with a wink, inferred more by the way she tilted her head a little with a smile rather than by only closing one eye. "Hey kiddo. Just been talking with your owner about some big girl stuff. Nothing for a cute little girl like you to worry about." I heard the rustling of vines as Mummy took off somewhere.

"Oh. Yeah. You're staying with us for a bit, aren't you?" I said with a bit too much excitement, attacking her with a violent hug. Penny was so soft, almost as nice as Mummy was. I just wanted to bury my face into her skin and cuddle her forever.

"Hey, you weren't meant to know that just yet! Did someone read Ella's messages, you naughty girl?" I could tell by her easy tone and boop on my nose that she was teasing.

"No! Well! Yeah…" I sighed. "There was this really bad feeling I woke up. Like something was very wrong. So I went to check what was wrong and…" I tilted my head away, only for it to be caught with a swift finger and dragged back to her.

"Hey. Kiddo. I understand this once, just don't do it again, okay? Either me or your Mummy will be here to explain those things if you need it from now on."

"Okay… sorry," I returned with a feather-soft voice.

"You're a good girl, Gaia! I can understand why, just… don't make it a habit, yeah?" She patted my head a few times as she finished. "Ella needs her space, you need yours."

Before I could continue moping, Mummy had come up behind me to slip my collar around my neck. I felt my heart skip at that, the pressure of the silk around my neck… it was heavenly.

I was a good girl. I was a pet. All my problems were going to be solved by my owner, and that was perfect. It was safe. It was warm. I let my mind cast itself into the bliss of total security that came with being a floret.

"You… weren't kidding, huh? You trained her into pissing herself when she gets excited." Wait, that was happening?! I whimpered, my face burning bright with shame.

"Much like domesticating her, it was rather impressively easy." Mummy's vine ran along my thigh, briefly soaking in the urine that I had spread down my legs and panties. "It's why I made sure this hab had all hard floors other than the play areas. I didn't want little miss pants wetter here soaking into the carpet, now."

"Hey, I-" I attempted a protest, but it was cut short by her voice.

"Sssssh, little one. The grownups are speaking." Just like that, I felt my desire to speak and defend myself shatter like flimsy glass thrown against a wall.

"Yeah. I was her 'owner' before you, as much as a terran can own another, and she's such a good girl it's adorable." Penny slid a finger underneath my collar, yanking it to pull me close to her. Then, she put a kiss on my forehead. "Even called a floret a few times in bed before… y'know." She rotated her hand around while giving a broad shrug.

"Oh trust me, I know." The two of them laughed, heartily.

"Few girls I know want to be called a floret before they end up actually domesticated. Happy to oblige them, of course." Penny smirked at me. "What a seed, though. I should've just taken Gaia to the domestication centre myself!"

"She's where she belongs now, with the woman that she belongs to. Isn't that right, Gaia?" Both of them were looking at me! I rose in their gaze, elated by their attention.

"Yes, Mummy!" I trembled with excitement. I was just glad that my bladder was empty or else I might have… stars. "Erm… can I get a nappy on now, Mummy? It feels unsafe to be in big girl underwear…"

There was a short interlude between getting dressed and actually going out for the night, so Penny took me to get coffee with her.

Of course, I wasn't going to have a big girl drink like that. Penny ordered for me and told me I was having a hot chocolate with extra marshmallows and cream. She even fed the cream to me!

As our drinks began to swirl down, Penny offered me a heavy sigh. I couldn't quite gleam her expression, though it was somewhere between concern and love.

Well, love was very easy to tell from her face! But as much as she might love me, there was something else biting at the back of her mind.

"By the way, kiddo… how much of Ella's messages did you read?"

I sunk a little in my chair, averting my gaze. "Am I about to get told off…?"

"No, no." She squeezed a hand on my shoulder, dragging my attention back to her with a kind finger on my cheek. That was twice today she did that! "I just need to know how much you know about what happened with Sarah."

"I know that she had a meeting with Mummy. I know that meeting went bad, and now she's got a wardship lined up or something? Iunno." Even if I was being kept at eye level, I wasn't going to keep eye contact.

"Y'know how sometimes they do wellness checks on people? Lana did a simple test to see how feral Sarah is. A test she failed with flying colours." She shook her head with a loud laugh. "Offered her coffee, she not only didn't accept coffee, she called Lana a weed."

"How could she?!" I gasped.

"Dunno. I'm not a feralist. Either that, or I can intuit that an affini offering me coffee means that I'm being tested on some axis. Either on accepting help, or politely being able to refuse help."

There was a brief silence. I pondered how I managed to last six months before getting collared, before returning to sadness about lost time. I could've had this sooner, but…

"Hey! Kiddo, stay with me." Penny snapped her fingers, returning me sharply to focus. "What I'm saying is that our little feralist in the friend group is going to be on edge. I'd be careful about what I say around her."

"Ah… Do you think I might upset her by being a floret?"

"Not much we can do about that. Both Sarah and Ella got so obsessed about the idea of not getting domesticated that they're both in this situation. I think there's some weird form of comradery between those two?" I squeezed my nails against my palm.

Why couldn't I be her? Why was out in front when my very existence was bad? I was ruining things, I was making it worse-

"Gaia. Babygirl, c'mon." She kissed my cheek like a guided missile to my heart. "I'm not about to have you spiralling into negative thoughts because of her bullshit."

"But I'm the wrong sophont for this! Sarah wants Ella, not me…" Her attempt at holding me at eye level failed, my gaze slumping onto the ground again like a discarded plastic bag. "It's all my fault."

"What she'll get is you, my adorable girlfriend. And if she's got a problem with that, she's got a problem with me. Anyway! I know Jack has a few films he wants to show just you." She picked herself up from the chair, walking steadily to me before wrapping arms tight around my body.

This sinking sensation of dread filling my stomach like a rock.

How bad it was to be hated for being me, and not my superior headmate.

Or better yet, let's throw my door wide open so all the weeds can get in!" Sarah was shouting loud. It really hurt.

It wasn't like that, I didn't…

I was a bad girl.

I was a bad girl. I didn't deserve this happiness.

The wine really was excellent nowadays. No more cheap, unsophisticated shite straight from a meadery's arsehole. Of course, when you could compile it exactly, that was hardly an accomplishment.

Farewell from that independent woman, Sarah Victor.

When she wakes up, she'll have to rapidly adjust to being a floret."

I could hardly find words. A long silence simply passed, Lana not stroking my hair but likewise not having removed her hand from my head. I didn't have the strength to raise my legs up, to move to another part of the hab.

"Where's Penny?"

"Penny and Jack have gone out for a bit. She won't be gone too long, though." Lana moved her hand downwards, finding my arm to squeeze it tenderly. I didn't have the energy to resist right now.

"I really fucked up, huh?"

"No, not really. There's nothing that could be done about Sarah. She was always going to end up domesticated, one way or another. Did you know that she shouted at Gaia for wearing a collar?" Somehow, that was entirely believable and also made me furious with rage. Why was she such an idiot?

How badly did you need to fuck it up for Lana of all affini to say that?

"Hey. Erm." My mouth was dry as I tried to say the next part. "You mentioned the implant. Did you start growing yours yet?"

"As of last night, actually." Lana patted my shoulder. "It made sense. While you still are independent, I'm coming to the realisation that I need to have more control over that body if I should properly fulfil my obligations to Gaia." Still independent was a choice set of words, but I didn't care.

My best friend had just been compulsed, and I, an independent terran, was about to have haustoria in my spine.

"Now, you need to eat something before the day ends. Can you handle that yourself, or should I take care of it this once? This is an exceptional circumstance, I wouldn't take it as evidence you need to be a floret should you accept this help."

"No, I'm fine," I lied, shaking off her touch and positioning myself upright, kicking off the nappy as I did so. "You said it yourself, I'm independent. I'll be fine handling this myself."

After all, all I had to do was tell the compiler what I wanted, right?

Why did I need someone else doing that for me?

What kind of hopelessly dependant person needs helping doing that?

Or bathing for themselves, or clothing themselves, or…

By the time I had gotten into bed, I realised I had only eaten about half a pizza slice.

It was her favourite food, too. She would've loved eating pizza with me, just reminiscing about our college days or our next big plan. It's not like I felt bad for wasting food, the atomic compiler would decompile it in seconds and it's not like I'd have wasted anyone's time for not eating.

This was probably the last night before Penny moved in. I gazed at my cold, empty bed that had enough space for three people in it. At my messages with Sarah that I'd have to wait days to see another ping in.

Would Sarah, stripped of her feralism, still be her? How much can you remove from someone before they're a new person?

One last time I opened up Sarah's message logs, scrolling sorrowfully through all the memories that we had together. Opening up the media tab to see all our pictures, our vacations together, the time that we spent.

They'd never be the same. We'd never be the same after this.

<HellaElla> I love you
<HellaElla> I'm sorry

With that, I shoved my phone to the side and closed my eyes.

Please don't let me be the one who wakes up.

Please let it be Gaia. Let her have her fun. Let her be a happy little floret.

Not… me. A broken mess pretending to be an independent terran.

Chapter 8: Promises

Chapter Summary

Ella doesn't want to be here anymore.

Chapter Notes

CW for self-loathing.

That.. thing. It was growing. Upstairs. Definitely nestled away somewhere in Lana's room where I couldn't find it or destroy it. No, Lana would never keep it in something that I was capable of breaking. Affini technology vastly outstripped our mortal abilities to destroy them. Even at terminal velocity, I knew that the resulting force wasn't enough to possibly even damage the container it was held inside. It knew me, definitely. It knew where it was going to infest into.

Did it crave me? Did it lust for my body in the way only a predator can?

If the implant required growing, that meant that Sarah's captor had been planning this for at least a week, if not more. Was this coincidental timing? Gaia was taken about a week ago. Did our actions create greater scrutiny on us than we anticipated, leading to Gaia's domestication and then Sarah's compulsing? Perhaps we could've avoided this by being quieter. Moving slower.

There wasn't much point debating these hypotheticals with myself. The old life I had died the moment Gaia opened up her neck to Lana. Just like how you getting domesticated caused chaos for me, you asshole.

Chapter 13: Memories

Chapter Summary

Ella takes a trip down memory lane.

Chapter Notes

Hey, folks. This is a softer chapter than last, but that's not saying much given the arc. No additional warnings this time, have fun!

See the end of the chapter for more notes

It was weird dreaming. Rather, it was weird being concretely in front during sleep. Usually when sleep came for me, we'd be in a weird free-floating state of rotating darkness. One moment I'd be the one in front, then another moment it might be someone else for a long while, my consciousness cast onto the wind.

Still, when I opened my eyes to unreality, I found myself heavy. My limbs were uncooperative; requiring a herculean amount of effort to lift an inch.

I decided it simply wasn't worth it with the day I had.

My head was resting upon something comfortable. I could take solace in that. The uneven topology of the surface suggested to me that it was probably some kind of alien, possibly an affini.

Which meant that somehow, they had pierced my cognition in order to enter my dreams. Perhaps it was yesterday's trauma or, perhaps, the other two growing comfortable with Lana. Whatever it was, I was bound now by hazy dream logic.

Fear was an emotion that I was meant to be feeling currently, given that I was on the lap of an affini, but it simply did not arise. If anything, there was a numb sensation all over my body, less like medical numbness more the results of being utterly knackered.

"Don't try to move too much, pup. You need the rest." Another insistence on being a dog, but I'd know the voice of that bastard rapist. No, I was dreaming about Her.

Concerningly.

I attempted to speak, but a bark tumbled out of my mouth instead. Decidedly not my bark, I was utterly incapable at those. It was as if I was an actual dog, or at least one of those puppygirls who had spent far too much time practising it.

Another attempt at speaking. Another bark.

"Puppy, I know you're excited to be here, but you really need to rest. You had a big, scary day." Stroking over my fur in slow motions, She continued to instruct me. "Just relax. Breathe. Let those bad feelings unwind from you."

Maybe one day, I'd love Her back.

It was peaceful, and the beer was nice.

"Did you get what you needed from this, petal?" I was briefly tempted to try another pet name with the same letter, but discarded that thought as quickly it entered.

"Do you want me as your floret?" she responded, sharply.

"I already feel pretty justified in that, Lana." With that, she turned her gaze away from me. Oof. That stabbed deep in my core. I'd be fine though, I could saunter on just like I've always done.

"Then what good does my opinion matter to you?" I sharply pointed back, hiding my own pain in my voice. "Besides. I made you a promise I wouldn't. Are you suddenly wanting me to break that promise?"

"I…" Ella slumped her shoulders forward. "I don't know. I don't know I don't know I don't know!" she turned to me with tears welling at the corner of my eyes. "I'm scared and everything is getting too much and I just! I just want to go back to two weeks ago!" The tears flew freely now, little sniffles accompanying them.

I remembered what Alexi said. I just had to… I had to take that step, I had to reach out into the unknown and know there was something waiting for me. No doubt they'd tell me now was the time to tell her that I was going to domesticate her. To pick up her worried parts of her mind and make them mine, keep them safe, where they couldn't be hurt.

Instead, I stumbled for a compromise. I picked Ella up and wrapped my arms around her, pressing her into my chest. The girls loved the this spot, my girls. Each one of them felt calmed when they were there.

"I hate you…" she eviscerated me with her words.

"I love you," I returned. I loved her more than she could ever know. "Let's get you home, little one."

"This was my home… until…" she sniffled, and I caught her tears in a vine.

"Yes, petal. But home is where your girlfriend is, isn't it? Not here."

I knew she had realised what that meant. Perhaps the realisation came earlier during her discussion with Ye-Jun, perhaps now as I said it, but it was causing fresh damage to her now.

Either way, my tongue had slipped. I should keep my designs for Penny quiet, but she was just too smart. She had figured it out already.

She didn't make an attempt at speaking. It was as if she was a doll; perfectly still and silent. But that's not what my Ella was meant to be. She was meant to be my beloved puppy.

She even let me undress her and dress her into her night clothes, her body still frozen with shock. I wanted to feel pride in having made this step, in being able to do this for her, but my core bristled with disgust at myself for this.

I took the collar off too, watching her body convulse in pain as she continued to feel the effects of what Daphne did to her.

Finally, I injected Ella with her sleeping medication, following swiftly suit with Penny, before grabbing them both like plushies to cuddle into myself.

I was a coward. And I'm starting to think Ella's hatred of me was rubbing off on myself.

Chapter 16: Parallel // Woah, we can talk to each other? // I GET TO RIDE A HORSE?

Chapter Summary

The girls finally get to talk to each other.

Chapter Notes

No content warnings, just a lot of arguing, weird dream logic, and an age regressor swearing.

Oh, and a certain horse.

I never thought I'd be this close to the stars.

Well, this wasn't actually space; I was dreaming. Obvious, given that I was standing in the vacuum of space without so much as a spacesuit with no negative side effects. In fact, I was as naked as the day I was born. Likewise, some sort of localised gravity field kept my feet on what appeared to be some sort of brown rock, or asteroid. Honestly, being this close to the celestial blanket of stars was relaxing.

I always held this bizarre reverence for space, ever since I was young. Terra was a dying rock crushed by humanity's greed, but out there? There could be anything. Species we've not heard of, fascinating planets and biomes that could only be dreamt of in my wildest imaginations. Then it turns out the stars had their eyes on us, and… well.

Turns out the stars didn't really care what we wanted.

I'd heard of the ships the Affini had, massive structures that were at once giant people carriers and armoured war ships, back when they had enemies to crush. I've always wanted to visit one, but decided against that since… well, everything I had was here.

I briefly considered that even if I still desired to visit one of those ships, it'd require me asking Lana for permission like I was a fucking incapable child, then sighed.

My hands were normal, or at least there was nothing unusual about them. I did notice there was a certain haze to them, briefly shimmering into paws if I considered it. Thankfully, they snapped back if I focused on it.

Perhaps I had the capability to decide my form here. Or perhaps that was illusory too. I dared not speak in case a bark was to form from my mouth instead of the words I wished to summon.

I instead kept walking on the asteroid path that created itself for me. I couldn't fall off and into the stars beneath, no matter how janky or sudden my movement was. Typical Affini rules, only they could hurt us. No doubt even if I threw myself right onto the asteroids, they'd create a softer one for my face to land on.

How dare you ruin even space for me?

A… forest?

Oh, that makes sense! I'm dreaming! I remember just being knocked out by that nice lady. Still, to think Mummy would create such a beautiful dream for me to have such a big nap! I looked down at myself, noticing the smaller body and frame. I was acutely aware of how big I was in the waking world since I despised it. Yet now, I was cute and dainty and most importantly, correct! Though my boobs were still pretty big, thankfully. I mean, the other girls probably wouldn't like this body so I hope this isn't permanent but it's nice for the now!

It was an aggressive terraforming project, since trees didn't make money and the Accord had no use for them. Now nature is gonna flourish everywhere!

Mummy wanted me to be there. Mummy wanted to hold me, even now.

"Waaaait, does that mean I'll go through withdrawal if I spend a while without you? That's lame." I groaned, dramatically.

"Jin, I'm in your spine. You don't have to worry about that, even if I was planning to let you out of my sight for extended periods." She patted my head again. How am I going to execute my plans for world domination now if Mummy doesn't leave me alone?

I.. guess I didn't want to be left alone from her for too long. She's the first woman who really gave a shit about me.

"Anyway, why Gaia? She the one with the most pull over her or something? Never mind, just get her here, it's lonely without her."

"You know, you're almost making it sound like I'm not enough, Jin." Aha, I had mortally wounded her! She was sounding all sad with her voice, which meant that I was the one who was in charge here! "Sad Mummy."

"Yeah? Lemme call you—" I was interrupted by more kebab in my gob.

"Not a chance, pet."

It was bathtime, so I got to look at myself totally naked in the mirror again. I was, of course, 'mirin myself. "So, how did you even do this? Making me look like this?!"

"Oh, it's pretty simple, actually. Basically, your body is now heavily modified to the point where the limbs adjust and shrink depending on who's out. Same with that hair. Usually shrink, considering that you're my big strong boy and have the largest frame." I flexed at that, adoring Mummy's doting attention on my arms.

"Damn right I am! Waaaait a second, does this mean I'm a cyborg?" I snapped my head to her, eyes burning with delight. "Does this mean I'm now… MECHA-JIN?!" I roared in delight at the concept.

Mecha-Jin, version 1.0, the unstoppable uncontrollable runaway train of a woman! The Jin Experience has been enhanced, augmented, upgraded! I transcended time, now I'm transcending the limits of flesh!

"Absolutely. There were a lot of cybernetics that went into making your body what it is now. Your nervous system—"

"My alert system, Mummy." I hit my signature pose and interrupted her. "Ever since the implant, I'm no longer nervous or scared, I'm alert and ready to rock." I punched the air. No explosion this time, tragically.

Mummy tittered. "Yes, your alert system and organs are what remain of your original body. Everything else got altered to better fit my girls' needs. Gaia gets so very small and cute, Ella gets puppy parts, and you get to be my big strong cyborg. Speaking of, I'm taking you running later. I've got another surprise in store for you."

I liked surprises. No doubt I was gonna burn down the track if she put me there. Yo, did they have floret races? Maybe I should race other florets and see if I'm faster than them.

I stared at my cybernetic body, before realising something important. Wait a second, if I was made of metal…

"Yo, Mummy, how can you wash a cyborg in the bath? Ain't I made of wires and machinery now?"

"Oh, Jin. Don't be so ridiculous." Mummy leaned forward and kissed my cheek. "Us Affini can do anything."

"Even wash a cyborg? Wow you guys are cool. I'm hitting that floret shampoo though, especially with this long hair!"

"Of course, Jin. Anything for my good boy." Fuck, that felt good.

Fuuuuck, I think I was in love with my mother.

Chapter 19: Apocalypse

Chapter Summary

"You knew how this story would end, didn't you? We both knew from the day we met. Yet we both hoped for something other than this."

Chapter Notes

I'm not adding too many tags/warnings for this since they'd spoil too much.

However, I will warn you that this chapter prominently features existential horror and religious trauma. It's going to be a rough one, but I promise there's salvation at the end.

Content Warnings if you really need them!:

Blasphemy, religious trauma, gaslighting, panic attacks, indentity death/compression, lying, gaslighting, existential horror, absolutely no consent, constant screaming, the most feral dog you've ever seen, IQ dropping. This is absolutely horror, I know, but there's a happy ending I promise.

Get ready for Ella's apocalypse.

See the end of the chapter for more notes

Why me?

Why must it be me again?

Had I not suffered enough the past few days, the past week? Have you not had your fill, you sick fuck?

My body had betrayed me, sending me out like a sickly doe with a predator loose.

No, this wasn't my body anymore. It was someone else's plaything. Perhaps the woman I despised most could now control who was fronting, who was dealing with her currently. I was merely a puppet, something to manipulate into whatever shape she so desired. She had replaced my nervous system with something more befitting her designs.

Evil. Vile. Loathsome. She was beyond contempt, yet what was I to do? Hating her was pointless. Were this a just world, I could be effective in my contempt. Justice was a faint memory for people like me.

People. We had long since forgotten that word, eschewing it in favour of calling ourselves 'sophonts' like that was any better. We surrendered our very humanity to these monsters, adopting their terms for us. Some particularly traitorous individuals did it willingly.

Apparently she had changed her appearance since I had been punched out. Why did she look like that? Why was she attempting to look like a kindly older lady rather than the hideous monster she is?

I scowled at her, and in response the bitch snapped a collar around my neck.

"Aren't you meant to only put that on when I'm going outside? To keep me safe from the monster you set after me?" I hissed.

"No, petal. You wear this collar all the time, now," Lana instructed, snatching me close onto her chest with a series of vines wrapped around me to hold me still. Infuriatingly soft noises littered my head, noises I refused to let comfort me. "We haven't been quite honest with each other, have we?"

I made a noise approximating confusion. "Fuck you mean we? You're the one who's been lying to my face for two fucking weeks, Lana!"

"Well, yes," she sighed like she was being tortured. "To be blunt, I've made quite the mess of everything, haven't I? I've hurt you more deeply than I hoped to ever hurt anyone, but especially someone I love so much."

Don't you fucking dare pretend you love me, you disgusting abomination.

"I apologise, but I also must insist upon honesty from now on. I'm going to domesticate you." Even the mention of domestication was enough to slice me open, the wounds from this ordeal bursting open.

"Domesticate me? But you promised me I'd be independent, you fucking monster!"

"I did, but I've had to break that promise," she informed me, her words as hard as steel with equally as much force behind them. "You aren't independent and you won't ever be. And honestly, letting you believe so for so long was a grave mistake on my behalf."

"So that's it? You're just going to break that promise to me?!" my mind dashed, frantically scouring my mental files for anything that could get me out of this situation. Wait, I was implanted, did this mean I was having my thoughts pruned? Could she read them? Did she know what I was thinking? Could she manipulate these thoughts?

No, this implant wasn't working. I was stronger than their bullshit tech, if it wasn't for those two fucking freaks I could rip it out of my spine no problem. Not that she'd let me, she'd never let me make a positive change in my life anymore.

"I've been lying to two people about your independence," Lana sighed again, somehow heavier than the last. "First of all, I've been lying to you, telling you that it's possible for you to avoid being my pet when you share a body with Gaia. And then there's the lie I've been telling myself, that I could resist becoming attached to you despite your proximity to the one I loved so dearly."

This was exactly what I've been waiting to hear. Those exact words, for her to come clean with how much she wanted to break that promise to me. Yet for some reason, the expected relief did not cascade over me, nor did my body unclench from its agitated pose. My fingers were still balling into claws, digging into the flesh, ready to unleash a punch like I had done to that asshole Violet.

"You should've domesticated me and Sarah together!" I spat. "In fact, you should've just left me the fuck alone!" I went for said punch, only for my hand to be grabbed by her hand.

"No, Ella. I should have domesticated you the first time you fronted after I took Gaia," Lana confessed, shaking her head. "There were several other opportunities and I am loathe to say I neglected to take any of them. I apologise, but it's clear to both of us what the path from here is."

"W-Wait, but I don't want to be a floret!" I screamed in desperation, loud enough for my voice to burn in agony. "Then all this suffering, everything that happened to me—"

"Will be mine to carry, yes." Her words punched mine out of the air. "They are no longer your burdens. Much like you are, they will be my charge and my property." She slowly squeezed my shoulder, and I felt that hissing anger from earlier. I was gritting my teeth, the only action left to me. Maybe I should try biting my tongue off so she can't get her prize.

"I don't think you understand the depths of my hatred for you. I despise you. I hate you." I scowled, a bitter growl rumbling in my throat. "Wait, the fact that I can even say this…" The realisation wormed its way into my mind, a sick truth that made my stomach feel like a black hole.

"Means the implant isn't working on you, currently. I wanted to have one last conversation with you as equals. Where I can explain my mistakes. As… equal as an affini can be with a terran."

"So… it's going to work on me soon?" No, please no, please anything but this. Anger melted into dread in a snap, transmuted by a single realisation.

It was terrifying. I shivered violently on her, feeling in that second such unfathomable levels of disempowerment. She held a loaded revolver to my head, capable of pulling the trigger whenever she got her fill of my suffering.

She put her finger like the barrel on my forehead, lightly rubbing it downwards. "Whenever I say so. In that moment, your independence will become a faint memory. A relic of your past, something which will grow increasingly foreign to you, day by day. That beautiful thinking capability of yours will become my possession."

Lana closed her eyes for a good long while, then sighed like she hated herself more than I hated her right now. Even now, my vision clouded black with utter hopelessness, I could see her slumping, unravelling just a fraction. Her eyes tracked on mine uneasily, like I was a mutilated carcass.

"P-Please don't do this…" I begged, pleaded, fervently hoping in vain that there was some out, something that I could do to delay the apocalypse.

Yet we both know how this story ends, don't we? You and I both knew this from the moment we met, Lana. We both knew this was only going to end one way. Perhaps that's why I hated you.

"I love you. I love you so, so much, my beautiful pet," she said, in between what sounded like sniffles. In that moment, I felt the tendrils of the implant spike into my brain, scouring deep into the grey matter of my primitive, mortal mind. I screamed as loudly as I possibly could. My hands went to my head, the claws attempting to dig into the skull and YANK THAT FUCKING PARASITE OUT OF MY HEAD.

I needed to keep thinking, I needed to occupy myself with something otherwise I'll start drifting into thoughts of her. Something, anything, I had to do this I had to hold on, to hold tight enough onto the truth of who and what I was. My species, my name, my everything, I couldn't surrender it to Her!

I am Ella Lockheart… I am a terran… I…

I clasped my hands together, my mind frantically fleeing to something that I hadn't entreated since I was a child. I begun praying, knowing that divine intervention was the only thing that could save me now.

"O-Our Father, who a-art in Heaven…" Words begun to slur into one another, my tongue acting as an uncooperative lump of flesh in my mouth. It was twisting and shaking in ways that refused to obey me, my entire body quivering like I had been hit with ice water. "T-Thy…" Her, She was looking at me, growing more luminous and brilliant each second. I had to pray harder, I had to—

"T-Thy kingdom come, thy will…" Thy will… what was Her will, anyway?

"Ella… stop resisting." Her voice was a clarion, cutting through the haze of my despair. "Be a good puppy."

T-That… Her will was for me to be good. To be Hers.

My resistance vanished as I flopped against Her, laying into Her soft vines. It was as if someone had completely erased every thought in my mind, wiping it dry like one would a chalkboard for more thoughts to bubble to the surface. I couldn't really remember what I was thinking about, but when I looked at Her, She was smiling.

That meant that I was being good. After all, She'd tell me if I wasn't being good, right?

There was a lot of bad feelings in my body right now, but hugging Her made them start to melt away. Her touch was bliss on my scalp, which felt like I just had hurt it somehow? But why would I damage Her property like that?

"How do you feel, puppy?" Everything about Her voice was so divinely beautiful that I felt jubilant just from being in Her presence. That I could be so blessed to be with Her.

W-What have you done?

"I feel tired and… weird. Sorry, You never told me your name… I kept trying to ask You but I'd wake up before I knew," I whined. "It wasn't fair."

"My name?" She chuckled. "It's Mummy, little pup. I'm your owner."

Mummy, of course! Back when I was still having to pretend to be a terran, they'd sometimes dress me up in funny suits and make me attend Mass. They'd call that man we were worshipping Father, it only made sense that such a being so great and feminine would want to be called Mummy!

How wonderful that I could feel Her. That I could be graced with Her arms wrapped around mine, Her hand stroking over the top of my fur in soft semi-circles. It almost felt too much to be around Her, like I was staring directly into the sun.

The hurt was numbing down, thankfully. A bad bit of me was having weird thoughts about being in Her light, but they were shrivelling and wilting in Her attention.

WHAT HAVE YOU DONE TO ME YOU SICK FUCK?

You promised me that you wouldn't do this! YOU PROMISED ME YOU WOULDN'T MURDER ME!

"Mummy? Am I a good puppy?" That was the only thing on my mind right now. I didn't want to be a bad dog, and something told me that I had been a really bad dog recently. That I had hurt Mummy and did a lot of really bad things.

"Of course you are. Good puppies aren't independent." Mummy was doing something with Her voice that made me really fuzzy and warm and soft! I liked it when She did that. "And do you think you're acting very independent right now?"

I was currently nuzzling up to my Mummy's chest and squeezing onto Her like She was an anchor to reality itself. "I— I don't know."

"I apologise, puppy. I was asking you to think, and that's not very fair to do to a silly pup like you. You're being a very good puppy." The words felt like well-read scripture, a treasured line from a holy book. A hurty bit of me was worried about letting Her think for me, but then it too died in Her scouring brilliance.

"I love you, Mummy…" I didn't know how to express it. Words failed to describe the depth of adoration I felt for Her. She was going to scrape each awful piece of my brain clean from all those horrible thoughts, and I couldn't ever thank Her enough.

"I love you too, my lovely doggy." Her praise filled me with warmth replete. "Now, we need to get you breakfast and bathed, don't we?"

I can do it myself, you fucking weed!

"Oh! What's for breakfast?"

"Porridge. It's for poorly girls, after all."

Turns out Mummy had put paws on and a tail on me when I wasn't looking. I had paws and a tail now! Completely swappable, so if Mummy wanted me to have opposable thumbs I could.

They were really sturdy, too. They didn't even feel weird to use, it was as if I had been using them my whole life! No doubt whenever She swapped into a more terran form, it'd be just as easy, or else She wouldn't do it!

I have a tail now? A-And ears?! Please, please don't do this, we can talk about this just don't degrade me like this!

Another bad thought. Another shake of my head and look at Mummy and away it went.

The porridge was served to me in a doggy bowl, and I tilted my head at Mummy. "Mummy, I think—"

"And did I say you could do that, puppy?" I whined at that, wincing at the idea that I had possibly disappointed Mummy. "It's okay, though. I forgive you. Dogs eat their food from the floor, not on the table like a person does."

That made a lot of sense. She tied my long hair back into a neat ponytail (or would that be puppytail?) before letting me eat the food. It was really scrumptious, too! The flavour of porridge was enhanced whenever you added cinnamon and honey into it, even if I wasn't too experienced at eating with my tongue. I really made a mess!

Was this how Sarah felt? I'm so sorry, Sarah, I'm so fucking sorry for everything.

"Of course, if you've been a good enough puppy to be allowed opposable thumbs, I might let you eat at the table." She chuckled, continuing to scratch my fur, just behind my ears. I was so glad that I had pointy doggy ears and a tail now, because that meant I could wag it whenever She was pleased with me! Or whenever She was around!

That praise made me melt into a puddle. "I love you Mummy!" I declared.

Walkies, yay!

PLEASE DON'T DO THIS. ANYTHING BUT WALKING ME AROUND LIKE A FUCKING ANIMAL.

YOU'RE ALL EVIL. EACH ONE OF YOU WEEDS DESERVES TO BURN FOR WHAT YOU'VE DONE TO ME, FOR WHAT YOU'VE DONE TO HUMANITY.
